का० प्रा० 748 : - --बैंककारी विनियमन अधिनियम , 
1949 ( 1949 का 10 ) की धारा 53 द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों 
का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीर सरकार, भारतीय रिजर्व बैंक की 
सिफारिश पर , एतदद्वारा, यह घोषणा करती है कि उक्त अधिनियम 
की धारा 19 की उपधारा 2 के उपबंध गिरवीदार के रूप मे 
टिवोली अपार्टमेंटस प्राइवेट लिमिटेड की . शेयर पूजी में 30 प्रतिशत 
से अधिक की शेयरधारिता के सम्बन्ध में , इस अधिसूचना के प्रकाशन 
की तारीख से 2 वर्ष की अवधि के लिए , यूनाइटेड बैंक माफ इंडिया 
कलकत्ता पर लागू नहीं होगें ।

S. O . 748 . --In exercise of the powers conferred by 
section 53 of the Banking Regulation Act , 1949 ( 10 of 
1949) , the Central Government , on the recommendation of 
the Reserve Bank of India , hereby declares that the pro 
visions of sub - section ( 2 ) of section 19 of the said Act 
shall not apply to tho United Bank of India , Calcutta , for 
a period of two years from the date of notification in respect 
of its holding of stares in excess of 30 per cent of the paid - up 
sharo capital of Tivoli Park Apartments Pvt. Ltd , as pledgee.

( अप्राधिकृत अधिभोगियों की बेदखली ) अधिनियम , 1971 
( 1971 का 40 ) को धारा 3 द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का 
प्रयोग करते हुए , नीचे दी गई सारणी के स्तम्भ III में 
उल्लिखित ऐसे अधिकारियों को , जो सरकार के राजपत्रित 
अधिकारी हैं , उक्त अधिनियम के प्रयोजन के लिए सम्पदा 
अधिकारी नियक्त करती है, और प्रागे निदेश देती कि 
उक्त अधिकारी अधिकारिता की स्थानीय सीमाओं के भीतर 
उक्त सारणी के स्तंभ ( 2 ) में विनिर्दिष्ट सरकारी स्थानों 
की बाबत , उक्त अधिनियम द्वारा या उसके अधीन सम्पदा 
अधिकारी को प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग और अधिरोपिन 
फर्तव्यों का पालन करेंगे ।

उपखंड ( ii ) में दिनांक 1982 - 09- 04 को प्रकाशित अधिसुचना संख्या एम . ओ . 3098 दिनांक 1982-(08- 01 में आंशिक संशोधन 
करते हा भारतीय मानक संस्था अधिसूचित करती है कि जल, गैस और मलजल के लिए ढलवां लोहे के दो फलैज वाले क्षतिज 
पाइपों की मानक महगे का डिजाइन संगोधित किया गया है । संबंधित भारतीय मानक के शीर्षक सहित मानक महरों के संशोधित 
विजाइना तथा उनके शाब्दिक विवरण निम्नलिखित अनुसूची में दिए गए है । 

भारतीय मानक संस्था ( प्रमाणन महर) अधिनियम 1952 और इसके अधीन बने नियमों तथा विनियमों के निमिस ये 
मानक महरें 1986- 01 - 01 से लागू होगी :

dated 1982- 09- 04, the Indian Standards Institution, hereby. notifies that the design of the Standard Mark 
for horizontally cast iron double flanged pipes for water gas and sewage has been revised . The revised designs of 
the Standard Marks together with the title of the relevant Indian Standard and verbal description of the design are 
given in the following Schedule.

अजित किया जाता है ।

उपयोग के अधिकार का अर्मन ) अधिनियम , 1962 ( 1962 का 50 ) 
की धारा 3 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन भारत सरकार के पेट्रोलियम एवम 
प्राकृतिक प्रेम मंत्रालय की अधिसूचना का . प्रा . सं . 2280 तारीख 
28- 5- 6 द्वारा केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उम अधिसूचना से संनग्न अनुसूची में 
विनिर्दिष्ट भूमियों में उपयोग के अधिकार को पाइप लाइनों को बिछाने के 
लिए अजित करने का अपना प्राशय घोषित कर दिया था । 

मो - यत: सक्षम प्राधिकारी ने उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उप 
धारा ( 1 ) के अधीन नरवार को रिपोर्ट दे दी है । 

और पागे, यत. केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उक्त रिपोर्ट पर विचार करने के 
पश्चात इस भधिनूचना से संलग्न अनुसूची में यिनिर्विष्ट भूमियों में उपयोग 
का अधिकार अजित मारने का विनिश्चय किया है । 

प्रय, अतः उरत अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रवत्त 
शक्ति का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय मण्यार एसद्वारा घोषित करती है कि 
इस अधिसूचना में गंलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट उक्त भूभियों में उपयोग 
का अधिकार पाइपलाइन बिछाने के प्रयाजन के लिए एतदद्वारा अर्जिन 
किया जाता है । 

और प्रार्ग उस धारा की उपधारा ( 4 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का . 
प्रयोग करते हुए मेन्द्रीय सरकार निर्देश देती है कि उक्त ममियों में उप 
योग का अधिकार केन्द्रीय सरकार में निहित होने की बजाय तेल और 
प्राकृतिक गेम मायोग में , ममी बाधाओं से मात कप में , घोषणा के 
प्रकाशन की इस तारीख को निहित प्रोगा ।

का . प्र . 787 – यतः गेट्रोलियम और खनिज पाइपलाइन ( भूमि में 
उपयोग के अधिकार का प्रर्जन ) अधिनियम , 1952 ( 1962 का 50 ) 
की धारा : को उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन भारत सरकार के 
पट्रोलियम और प्राकृतिक गैस मंत्रालय की अधिसूचना का प्रा में . 35 31 , 
नारीश्न 27- 09-86 मारा केन्द्रीय सरकार ने नम अधिसूचना से सलग्न 
अनुसूची में विनिदिष्ट भमियों में उपयोग का अधिकार को पाइपान । इनों 
को बिछने के लिए प्रजित करने का प्रपना प्राशय घोषित कर दिया 
था ।

sertion ( 1 ) of section 6 of the said Act, subritical report to 
the Government ; 


And further where is the Central Govenment has , after 
considering the said report decided to acquire the right of 
liser in the lands specified in the schedule appended to this 
notification ; 


और यत. सक्षम प्राधिकारी ने उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की 
उपधारा ( 1 ) के प्रवीन मरकार का रिपोर्ट दे दी हैं । 

और प्रागे , यतः फेन्द्रीय सरकार ने उक्त रिपोर्ट पर विचार करने के 
पश्चात् इस मधिसूचना मे सलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट ममियों में उपयोग 
का अधिकार अजिम करने का विनिश्चय किया है । 


ब , न उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा 
प्रवन शक्ति का प्रयोग कर में हुए फेन्द्रीय सरकार एतदद्वारा घोषित करती 
है कि इस अधिसूचना में संलग्न अनसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट उक्त भूमियों में 
उपयोग का अधिकार पाइपलाइन बिछाने के प्रयोजन के लिए एतददाग 
प्रजित किया जाता है ।

sub - section ( 1 ) of the Section 6 of the said Act, the Cent 
ral Government hereby declares that the right of user in 
The said lands specified in the " chedule appended to this 
notification hereby acquired for laying the pipeline;

29- 3- 88. द्वारा केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उस अधिसूचना से संलग्न अनुसूची 
में विनिर्दिष्ट भूमियों में उपयोग के अधिकार को पाइपलाइनों को बिछाने 
के लिए अजित करने का प्रपना प्राशय घोषित कर दिया था ।

( 1 ) के अधीन सरकार को रिपपोर्ट दे दी है । 

और पागे , यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उक्त रिपोर्ट पर विचार करने के 
पश्चात इस अधिसूचना से संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट भूमियों में उपयोग 
का अधिकार अजित करने का विमिरपय किया है । 

प्रय, अतः उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उप -धारा ( 1 ) द्वारा 
प्रवस शक्ति का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार एतद्द्वारा घोषित करती 
है कि इस प्रधिसूचना में संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिविष्ट उक्त भूमियों में 
उपयोग का अधिकार पाइपलाइन बिछाने के प्रयोजन के लिए एतदद्वारा 
अजित किया जाता है । 

और मार्ग उस धारा की उपधारा ( 4 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग 
करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार निर्देश देती है कि उक्त भूमियों में उपयोग 
का अधिकार केन्द्रीय सरकार में निहित होने की बजाय सेल और प्राकृतिक

" The Mysore High Court had vory rightly observed that

nor the unconstitutional practice of making an un 
justifiable iscrizination in promotionul chances of 
Government servants belonging to what was really 
a single category , without any reference either to 
merit or seniorly, or educational qualifications, could 
justify the differences in promotional chances . . . ,

THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : MARCH 21, 1987 / PHALGUNA 30 , 1908 [PART II Sec . 3 (1 ) ] 


AWARD 
The Goveromont of Indie, Ministry of Labour, by its 
Order No. L - 22012 / 13 /87- 1 . 111( D ) dated 12 - 7 - 1984 referred 
the following dispute under Sections 7A and 10 ( 1 )( d ) of the 
Industrial Dispules Act , 1947 belwcen the ediployers in re 
lation to the management oi M / S, Singareni Collieries Coin 
pany Limited . Ramagundam Division - U and their workman 
10 this Tribunal for adjudication : 


"Whcther the management of Messrs Singareni Collie 

ries Co . Ltd ., Ramazundam Division -ll are justified 
in reverting Sri Enugu Chandroiul , Tub Repairing 
Mistry , GDK SA Inc ine from Categrity V to Cat. 
IV ? If not, to what relief is the workinan , concerned 
entitled ?" 


On completion of three young on par with Tradesmen . There 
foro Adiother order was issued by cancelling promotion by 
a specific order . Tho workmai who was promote erroucously 
from Cat. IV to Cat. V purported to be based on the circular 
referred above cannot get i vosted right to continue as the 
vaid circular has no application in his case and it relates 
only to Tradesmen and the workman who is Tub -Rcpairing 
Maistry does not come under the Category of Tradesmen . 
For the first time cadre scheme for Trudesinen was evolved 
in the year 1976 wherein it was specified that all the Trades 
men with threo years service in Category IV ( except Masons, 
Tub -Repairing /Making Maistries and it Grinders ) will be 
eligible for promotion to Category V the cffect of it is that 
the Cadre Scheme is not applicable to the Tub -Repairing / 
Making Maistry . They are specifically excluded from cate 
gory of tradesmen . There are number of job repairing and 
making maistries who have put in longer service in dispute 
and any undue concession granted to a workman out of the 
way would result in multiple claime. Thereforc the Manage 
ment rectified this mistake. It is true that he worked for 
five months and Category V and was paid wages of Cat. V 
also the reversion is not by way of pedal action . So the 
action of the Management is only a correction of its own 
mistake and the same cannot be termed as wrongful, illegal 
and unjustified , 


This reference was registered as Industrial Dispule No. 42 
of 1984 and polices were issued to the parties. 


2 . The claims statement filed by the Workman of Singa 
reni Collieries Company Limited , Ramagundam Division - II 
requesting the cancel the revertion of Şri Enugu Chandraiah 
from Category V 1o Caiegory IV and to retain him in the 
promoted Category V . It is mentioned that E . Chandraiah . 
Tub repairing mistry GDK 5A Incline was promoted from 
Category IV to Category V with effcct from sth May 1983 
after giving him trade test. In llic letter of promotion given 
by the Management it was clearly mentioned that his con 
firmation would depend upco. the satisfactory conduct and 
report regarding the workman and attendance during the 
period of three months. Tlic Worknin in , dispute was pro 
moted with effect from 5th May 1983. On 9th August 1983 
the Management issued a leller stating that the promotion 
given to him is treated as cancelled and he is reverted 
back to Category IV wages . Thus the workman worked for 
more than five months without any bad remarks and in 
gpite of good work and a conduct and attendance he has been 
domoted without any chargc snact or warning letter to him . 
The action of the Management dcmoting lim from Cat. V to 
Cat. TV wages is wrongful, illegal and unjustified . As per the 
Company Standing Orders and also principles of natural jus 
tice the workman cannot be deinoted without any reasonable 
cause is shown during the period of prohibition , Thus the 
said reversion is wrongful, illegal and unjustified . 


6 . The reference was received in this Tribunal on 21- 7 - 84 
and notices of the same was received by both parties by 
17 - 8 - 1984 . Claims statement was received in Tribunal on 
17 - 8 - 1984 and Sri K . Srinivasa Murthy and Miss. G . Sudha 
and Sri H . K . Saigal flled vakalat and counter for the 
Management after five adjournments on 17- 1 - 1985. There 
afterwards Sri D . S . R . Varma, filled his vakalat for work 
men after six adjournments . Thereafterwards, finally the 
matter is posted for enquiry and on 6 - 8 - 1986 W . W . 1 is 
examined and Exa . W1 to W3 were marked and posted for 
workmay evidence is closed and it is posted for Manage 
ments evidence . In fact on the same day Sri B . Ganga Ram 
filed a Memo stating his unwillingness to come again 
for cross cxamination , the Management should pay to and 
fro charges . Actually the said Memo is numbered as M . P . 
No. 257 / 86 and on 1 - 9 - 1986 G . Sudha counsel for the Res

of W . W . 1 to 29 - 9 - 1986 , 


3 . The Management filed a counter stating that there is 
a specific job described and cotegorisotion for Tub - repairing / 
Making maistry in Coal Wage Board they are entitled for 
Category TV only . The jab rcscrintion of Tub - repairing / 
Making Maistries roads the following “ Blacksmith and their 
mazdoors generally do all tub -rcrairs including fastening the 
tub blocks to the frame with bolts and nuts " . This is written 
in the Wage Board in Category - IV (Skilled Junior ). The 
workmen concerned working in Tab repairing maistry in 
Category II and he was promoted as Tub repairing maistry in 
Category IV with effect from 16 - 1 - 1980 . 


4 . There is a cadre scheme for tradesmen like electricians 
and Fitters which are placed in Category IV , V and VI as 
per which the tradesmen who have completed three years 
of service in Category IV are considered for placement in 
Category V on the basis of assessment of their work and 
corduct. The Circular dt. 22- 8 - 1981 was issued by the Chief 
General Manager . Kothagudan to all the Mines and Denart 
ments informing that review of promotions from leenty 
IV to V for Tradesman can be done twice as on 1st March 
and 1st Sentemher in a year as against the carlier prari : 
of only once in a year ie , as on 1st March only . So the 
cadre scheme is specifically apnlicable to Tradesman only 
and not oorlichle to the Tub - repniring Maistrie who are 
not tradesmen .

" What is res judicata is the final decision on the matter 

directly and substantially in issue in the former 
proceedings. A matter which inight or ought to 
have been made a ground of attack Os defence in

operates as res judicata ( constructive res judicata ) 
in vicw of Explanation IV 1o $ . 11 Civil P . C . when 
a party fails to raise a particular plea which could

again . This doctrine of constructive res judicata 
was held applicable even to the decision rendered 
in writ notition under Art. 226 of the Constitution

14 . Similarly , the second decision which has been referred 
to is the State of Uttar Pradesh , v . Nawab Hussain 1977 Lab . 
I. C . ( TC ) pugc 911. In the said case also when the peti 
tioner was dismissed from service , he fled a petition for 
quashing the disciplinary proceedings on the ground that he 
was not afforded a reasonable opportunity to mcct the alle 
gations against him and the action taken against him was 
mala fide . The petition was dismissed. Thereafter he fled 
a suit in which he challenged the order of dismissal on the 
ground , inter alia , that he had been appointed by the I.G . P . 
and that Dy. I. G .P . was not comsetent to dismiss him by 
virtue of Art. 311 ( 1 ) of Constitution . It was held that the 
suit was barred by the principle of constructive rea judicata 
In my opinion , even if the question has been referred to this 
Tribunal, it is clear that tere is no industrial dispute which 
in fact, exist: d which could have been referred to this Tri 
bunal. The question as regards the sentence awarded to the 
concerned workman . it was already decided by the Hon ble 
High Court in the Letter Patent under appeal and it is not 
onen for thic Tribunal to suit in judgement against the orders 
of the Hon ble High Court. The question has been fully and 
positively dealt hy the Hon ble High Court and I do not 
think that it could he re - opened at this stage . It was urged 
that the Tribunal can re - appreciato and may come to a 
different conclusion . I do not think that it is now onen to 
the concerned employec who has thought fit to none the 
Hon ble High Court now to urge that I can re - appreciate 
the issue In my opinion . there is no substance in the argu 
ment urged ind hence I hold that the prosent reference is not 
competent as the isruc has already been decided hy the 
Hon ble High Court and hence I pass the following order :

Karmachari Sangh (hereinafter called Kalol Union ) and 
IFFO Karmachari Sangh , Phulpur (hereinafter colled Phul 
pur Union ).

3. In October , 1974 the management in relation to JFFCO 
reached tripartite settlenxent with workmen of its kandla 
unit, hend office and marketing division giving benefits of 
wage revision and other benefits. A similar tripartite settle

from 1 - 1 - 1973 to 30 -6 - 1977. 


4 . After 30 -6 - 1977 fresh tripartite settlements were arrived

Employees Union , Kandla on 28 -8 - 1979 , with the workmen

workmen of Phulnus Unit on 27 - 11 - 1979 . These settlements 
which may be called Second Tripartite Settlements were 
made effective from 1- 7 - 1977 to 30-6 - 1982, 


11 . In the meantime Third Tripartite Settlements were

on 21- 8 - 1985. Both the Third Tripartitc Settlement were 
made effective from 1- 7 - 1982 to 30 -6 - 1986 . Since then the 
workmen of Kandla Unit and the Head Office and Marketing 
Division at Delhi are not in the fray and have ceased to 
appear in the present proceedings to press any demand , 


5 . The workmen of Phulpur who were paid Dearness 
Allowance at Central Government D . A . Schenioweto 
brought under Industrial D . A . Scheme by the Second 
Tripartito Settlement. This change said to have adverse 
effect on many workmen is one of the causes of the prosent 
dispute . 


6 . The workmen of Karol Unit did not enter into Second 
Tripartitc Settlement after the expiry of the period of the 
First Tripartite Settlement on 1- 7 - 1977 as they did not agree 
to the change over proponod by tho management from 
Central Government D . A . Scheme to Industrial D , A 
Scheme. So the workmon of Kalol Unit continue to be 
governed by the same service conditions as were fixed by 
the Tripartito Settlement of Sth July , 1975 . Their basic 
wages remain the same since 1 - 7 - 1977 and they have been 
getting Dearness Allowance under the Central Government 
D . A . Scheme,

MW - 4 . Shri Tandan has admitted that prices of raw mate 
rials have increased from year to year during the last 10 
year s But I find that the wages of labour, of the con 
cerned workmen the prime mover of the industry have not 
kept pace with the increase in the cost of other ingredients . 
In this connection I may quote thic observation of His 
Lorushin Khalil J . of the Suprema Court in Workmen , 
Wadula Factory of LH . P . Co . Ltd . Vs. Indian Humo Pipe 
Co. Ltd . 1986 ( ab . JC 1644 at page 1654 " The sweat of 
labour is never reflected in any Balance Sheet although the 
latent force behind any successful industry is this sweat"

22 . The contribution of the Central Government to the 
Share Capital of IFFCO is about Rs 49 . 29 crores as against 
Rs. 38. 24 crores contributed by about 25000 agricultural co 
operative societies . The raw materials for manufacture of 
fertilisers are supplied to it at prices fixed by the Central 
Government. Solling prices of the fertilisers are also fixe : 
by the Central Government. The retention price in determined 
hy the Fertiliser Industry Co - ordination Committee cont 
rolled by the Central Government. The retention prices for 
cach manufacturing unit of the industry comprised their costs 
of production plus 12 % post tax return on net worth . The 
cost of production for inclusion in retention price is deter 
mined on the hasis of 80 % capacity utilisation of the ins 
talled capacity of the manufacturing unit , Subsidy is paid to 
IFFCO as difference between manufacturing costs and the 
sollirg nrices detarmined by the Centr : 1 Government. Areas 
are allocated for sales of the fertilisers from time to time by the 
Central Government. Freight compensation is paid for move . 
monl of fertilisers from the plants to the various selling points.

28 . In revising the wage structure the foll. wing general 
principles should he kept in view . The worker is entitled 
to fair wage the lowcr limit of which is minimum wage 
and tlie upper limit is set hy the spacity of the industry 
to pay . There should be a progressive movement towards 
living wige . Equal wages sliould be paid for equal work . 
wages should be fixed on industry - cum - region principle . 
Thus in fixing wage scales or dearn : ss allowanco or in rel 
sing the same, two principal factors must weigh with the 
adjudicator namely (1) how the wage prevailing in the 
establishment in question compare with those given to 
worknen of similar grade or scales ly sinular establishnients 
in the same industry or in their absence in similar establish 
ments in other industry in the region ; (ii) what thc estab 
lishment in question can pay without any undue strain on 
its financial resources. The former is the industry - 4107 -region 
principle and the latter is the factor of financial capacity . 
Determination or revision of wages should permit a fai: rc 
muneration to 1: bou . a fair return on the capital investet 
and should strengthen incentive to Miciency. Inter inciustry 
wage differential in the same region should not f:1r as 
nossible he permittent cycept on grounds of diferences in 
the standing, technology and capital ner worker . Emplovees 
in the public sector should be on th : whole helf rar with 
their counterparts 111 private employment. In order that a 
ware structure is properly determincat o revised it woull be 
just and equitable to take into considrratio The value of 
heneits which go directly to reduce aynenses of workers on 
items of pynenditurc which are tiken into count for cal 
culation of the fair ware . These henefith relating to wrlfarc 
measure are usually called fringa hefs The frince hene 
fits cannot he precisely values in terms of monev . They 
heln consulerithly in buidning gin liucen fair wipe and 
living war . Such fringe henefits are free medicul nii, child 
ren education allowance , suhsidised rantern facilitice rini 
form allowance, washing allowance , rent free piccommodation 
and like benefits.

हए उक्त स्थापन को , 27-11- 1985 से तीन वर्ष की

___ 1 . उक्त स्थापन के सम्बन्ध में नियोजन प्रादेशिक

. . - . 


देने से पूष फमचारियों को अपना दृष्टिकोण स्पष्ट करने का 
युक्तियुक्त अवसर वेगा । 


अनुसूची 
1. उक्त स्थापन के सम्बन्ध में नियोजन प्रादेशिक भविष्य 
निधि प्रायुक्त तामिल नाडु को ऐसी विवरणियां भेजेगा और 
ऐसे लेखा रखेगा तथा निरीक्षण के लिए ऐसी सुविधाएं 
प्रदान करेगा जो केन्द्रीय सरकार समय- समय पर निर्दिष्ट 
करे । 


2 . नियोजक , ऐसे निरीक्षण प्रभारी का प्रत्येक मास की 
समाप्ति के 15 दिन के भीतर सन्दाय करेगा जो केन्द्रीय 
सरकार, उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 17 की उप -धारा ( 30 ) 
के खण्ड ( क ) के अधीन समय - समय पर निर्दिष्ट करे । 


9. यदि किसी कारणवश, स्थापन के कमचारी , भारतीय 
जीवन बीमा निगम की उस मामहिक बीमा स्कीम के , जिसे 
स्थापन पहले अपना चुका है , अधीन नहीं रह जाते हैं , या 
इस स्कीम के अधीन कर्मचारियो को प्राप्त होने वाले फायदे 
किसी रीति से कम हो जाते है तो यह छूट रद की जा 
सकती है । 

10 . यदि किसी कारणवश, नियोजक भारतीय जीवन 
बीमा निगम द्वारा नियत तारीख के भीतर प्रीमियम का 
सन्दाप करने में असफल रहता है , और पालिमी को व्यपगत 
हो जाने दिया जाता है तो छूट रद्द की जा सकती है । 

11. नियोजक द्वारा प्रीमियम के सन्दाय में किए गए 
किसी व्यतिक्रम की दशा में , उन मत सदस्यों के नामनिर्द 
शितियों या विधिक वारिमों को जो यदि यह , छुट न दी 
गई होती तो उक्त स्कीम के अन्तर्गत होते , बीमा फायदों के 
सन्दाय का उत्तरवायित्व नियोजक पर होगा । 

12. इस स्कीम के अधीन पाने वाले किसी सदस्य की 
मुत्य होने पर शास्लीय जीवन बीमा निगम , बीमाकृत राशि 
के हकदार नामनिर्देशिती/विधिक वारिसों को उस राशि का 
सन्दाय तत्परता से और प्रत्येक दशा में हर प्रकार में पूर्ण 
दावे की प्राप्ति के एक मास के भीतर निश्चित करेगा । 

[ संख्या एस - 350 1 1/ 21) 82 पी . एफ - 2/ एस . एम - 2 ] 


3. सामहिक बीमा स्कीम के प्रशासन में , जिसके अन्तगत 
लेखाओं का रखा जाना, विवरणियों का प्रस्तुत किया जाना , 
बीमा प्रीमियम का सन्दाय , लेखाओं का अन्तरण , निरीक्षण 
प्रभारों का सन्दाय प्रादि भी है , होने वाले सभी व्ययों का 
पहन नियोजक द्वारा किया जाएगा । 
___ 4. नियोजक , केन्द्रीय सरकार द्वारा यथा अनुमोदित 
सामहिक बीमा स्कीम के नियमों की एक प्रति , और जब 
फभी उनमें संशोधन किया जाए , तब उस संशोधन की प्रति 
तथा कर्मचारियो की बहुसंख्या की भाषा में उसकी मुख्य 
प्रातों का मनु याद , स्थापन के सूचना - पट्ट पर प्रदर्शित करेगा । 

5. यदि कोई ऐसा कर्मचारी, जो कर्मचारी भविष्य 
निधि का या उक्त अधिनियम के अधीन छूट प्राप्त किसी 
स्थापन की भविष्य निधि का पहले ही सदस्थ है, उसके स्था 
पन में नियोजित किया जाता है तो नियोजक सामुहिक बीमा 
स्कीम के सदस्य के रूप में उसका नाम तुरन्त दर्ज करेगा 
और उसकी बाबत प्रावश्यक प्रीमियम भारतीय जीवन बीमा 
निगम को सन्दस करेगा । 

6. यदि साम हिक बीमा स्कीम के अधीन कमचारियों को 
उपलब्ध फ़ायदे बढ़ाये जाते हैं तो , नियोजक उक्त स्कीम के 
अधीन कर्मचारियों को उपलब्ध फ़ायदों में समुचित रूप से 
पद्धि की जाने की व्यवस्था करेगा जिससे कि कर्मचारियों के 
लिए सामूहिक बीमा स्कीम के अधीन उपलब्ध फ़ायदे उन 
फायदों से अधिक प्राकल हो , जो उक्त स्कीम के अधीन 
अन जय हैं । 
___ 7. सामूहिक बीमा स्कीम में किसी बात के होते हुए भी , 
यदि किसी कर्मचारी की मृत्यु पर इस स्कीम के अधीन 
सन्वेय रकम उस रकम से कम है जो कर्मचारी को उस दशा 
में सन्देय होती जब वह उक्त स्कीम के अधीन होता तो 
नियोजक कर्मचारी के विधिक बारिस नामनिर्देशिती का प्रति 
कर के रूप में दोनों रकमों के अन्तर के बराबर रकम का 
सन्दाय करेगा ।

को प्राप्त हुश्री था । 


The question , is the quantum of other work which was 
being done by the petitioner . The major work and dutics of 
the petitioner , as has been found by the Labour Court and 
with which I wholly concur was of convassing for the salo 
of the books of the respondent. 


The main work of the petitioner, was of securing orders 
and increasing the sale of the respondent and the clerical or 
manual work which he might have carried out was only in 
cidental to this . 


Thc petitioner had been rightly categorised as not being 
a workman 4 .1d as such the Labour Court was right in not 
granting any relief to thc petitioner."

BEFORE SHRI G . S . BAROT, PRESIDING OFFICER , 
INDUSTRIAL TRIBUNAL (CENTRAL ) AT AHMEDABAD 
REFERENCE (ITC ) No. 1 of 1982. 

Adjudication 

BETWEEN 
The Management of Bank of India, Ahmedabad . 

AND 

Their workmen . 
In the matter of demand for regularisation and permanent 
ribsorption of certain part-time cmployees, etc . 


It was further observed as under : 


APPEARANCES : 

Shri M . J. Sheih — for Bank of India . 
Shri T . R . Mislira — for the workmen . 
Shri P , S . Chari-- for Dave M , B ., an employee . 


The present case is similar to the case of May Baker 

India Limited y . Tlicir Workmen ( 1 ) . There it was 
held that if the nature of the duties is manual or 
clerical then the person must be held to be a 
workman , On the other hand , if the manual or cleri 
cal work is only a small part of the duties of the 
person concerned and incidental to his main work 
which is not manual or clerical, then such a pesson 
would not be a workman . In that case 3 person hud 
been employed by a pharmaceutical concern as a rc 
presentative for convassing orders and it was found 
that the work consisted mainly of convassing and 
the clerical or manual work that he did was only 
incidental to his main work . It was held by the 
Supreme Court that he would not be regarded as 
a workman . In the present case also the main work 
of the petitioner way of convassing for orders . It 
is only when the orders materialised and sale was 
made that the petitioner got his commission . The 
main work of the petitioner, therefore , was of se 
curing orders and increasing the sale of the res 
pondent and the clerical or manual work which he 
might have carried out was only incidental to this . 
To tie sane effe t is another decision of the Supremo 
Court in the case of Burmah Shell Oil Storage ana 
Distribution Company of India Ltd . v . The Buimah 
Shell Management Staff Association and others."

" The form of the order of termination or the languago 

in which !1 is couched is not conclusive . The court

II . 


The applicant was punished under the C . C . S , ( C . C . A .) 

Rules 1965, for specitic acts of misconduct Under 
their contract of employisent with the employer 
viz ., the General Manager , GCF , Jabalpur, he is 
bound to follow a certain code of conduct which 
could be expresses or implied. It is the infringement 
of this contract of employment, unilaterally , by the 
appellant, that resulted in disciplinary action against 
him . The disciplinary authority hos thus acted con 
pletely within his jurisdiction and he has nowhere 
infringed any rights of the applicant, constitution :21 
or legal or any other . The action of the disciplinary 
authority was in accordance with the due process 
laid down in CCS (CCA ) Rules, 1965. 
In view of the above facts , Shri R . S . Tripathi is not 
entitled to claim any relief." 


The Court will find out from other proceedings or 
documents connected with the formal order of 
termination what the true ground for the termina 

tion is . . 
A disciplinary inquiry resulting in punishment of particular 

delinquents cannot but bo illegal if the evidence is 
of mass misconduct by unspecified strikers led by 

leaders who are perhaps not even workmen . 
The workmen were on strikc. The strike was illegal. 

Thc Management was hurt because production was 
paralysed. The striker s allegedly inc ulged in objec 
tionable activties. The exasperated Management hit 
back by ordering noir discharge for reasons set 
Olt in several pages in tle appropriate contempo 
raneous proceeding. Miscorduct after misconduct was 

flung on the workers to justify the drastic action , 
Held : ...... The orders of discharge were bad on this 

score alone."

papers relied on by the management clearly go to show that 
this movement of the union members was directed against 
the General Manager , Shri R K . Ghai and he was at least 
one of the aggrieved party iind personally prejudiced against 
the active members of the unlon .

9 . On behalf of the management, it has been contended 
that active members of the Ui veni licyond the legitimate

may be true , but the law of natural justicc . requires that 
such activities of the union members or office bearcrs as 
are subversive of disciplinc amounting 10 misconduct ,hag to . 
be proved by legal evidence before a person could be punish 
ed . In the instant case , except the alleg . tions in the charge 
sheets there is nothing on record at least beforc this Tribunal 
to substentiate the allegations of the management. On the 
other hand , applicent workman lan fileul his own alidavit and 
he has been cross- exadlined ty the maruger.czt on it. But 
nothing material is brought out in his cross .exarination to 
discredit his plea in relation to ihe charges levelled agiiast 
him . If the management wanted to rehut the allegations 
of his affidavit it should have cilha filed the counter affidavit 
of Shri B , K . Ghai or at least of some responsible officer 
to refute or rchut the allecaliuns but nothing has been done . 
I, therefore , sec no reason o disbelieve the affilavit of th : 
apnlicant workman (Pratan Singh V s . State of Punjab - AIR 
1964 SC 72 para 14 relied on ). 


It has been further deposed that the above statement was 
obtained on misrepresentation and inducemcnt that if họ ad 
diits the charge ho will not be punished . As already pointed 
out there is no counter - affiduit to deny this allegation . It 
appears to be true also because in the body of reply to tho 
show cause notice dated 19 - 8 - 1981 Ex. W / 2 the workman 
had clearly stated that the strike was in pursuance of the 
decision and call of the G . C . F . Employees Union wilhout 
any ulterior motive or any desigu to subvert discipline of 
the authority of the Geacral Manager , Gun Carriage Factory . 
This firstly goes to show that the plea of the workman was 
attained on misrepresentation and inducement, is true . Sec 
condly it further goes to show that it was not an admission 
of guilt. Therefore it could not have been taken as such as 
has been held in the case of Jagdish Prasad Saxcna Vs. State 
of M . P . {AIR 1961 (MP ) p . 1070 ]. As per charge sheet Ex, 
W / S , W / 10 , W / 14 , W / 18 , W22 and Ex , W /27 there 
was no such admission yet the disciplinary authority did not 
consider it necessary to conduct a Comestic enquiry in pre

got the documents proved . The record does not disclose that 
any of the documents or record applied fo : by the workman 
was supplied to him before finding him guilty . In fact,

workman has stated that he did not receive the chaigo sheet

the reminder. But this objection also errears to have been

follows 


Under this Rule Shri B K . Ghai should have referred the 
matter to the Government for appointment of an ad hoc 
disciplinary authority to conduct just, legal and impartial 
domestic enquiry against the worknian but instead of doing 
60 he himself became the prosecutor and judge at the same 
timo, It is now well settled that no person could be a 
judge in his own cause and no witness could testify that 
bis own , testimony is true us has been held in the case of 
1980 - II-LL p . 270 P . J. Harkali Vs. K . B . Karamjkar; 1986 
SLR ( 1 ) 558 S . Tiwari Vs. State of MP ( 1984 MPLJ 516 , 
This is what has been done by Shri 11 . K . Ghai. Thereforo 
the entire procedings are vitiated and contrary to law .

15 . Consequently I hold that the action of the manage 
ment of Gun Carriage Factory , Jabalpur (MP) in punishing 
the workman Shri R . S . Tripathi by orders of penalties dated 
6 . 5 ,81, 6 - 7 -81, 26 - 12 -81, 19 -4 - 82 , 4 -5 - 82 . 22-6 -82 , 
2 -5 -83, 27- 7 -84 , 1- 8 -84 and 19 - 1 - 85 for his trace union 
activities is unfair labour practice and unjustified . The work 
man is therefore entitled to all hus dues / claim 15 if the 
above orders were not passed . No order as to costs.
